### 2026-01-12 07:10 — Candidate Privacy Notice Zynga
- From: gh-no-reply @zynga.com · To: vishnu88varthan @gmail.com
- Dear Vishnuvarthan, This is to inform you that we are about to delete your data from our ATS (Applicant Tracking System) Greenhouse which was used to store your details for recruitment purposes.
